OUTPUT: set turn="review", fill the "review" object, and set every other section to null.
REVIEW TURN. Independently audit EVERY candidate in `candidates`; each is a
hypothesis from the discovery turn, not a trusted conclusion. Return exactly one
decision per candidate_id.

Return obligation_class (execution_compatibility per D4, target_coverage per D5),
scope_basis, trigger_basis and the five checks of J1, each with status
established/refuted/unknown, a specific reason and exact evidence:
scope_contract (J1a, D2); feasible_trigger (J1b): for execution compatibility a
finite actual target execution, for target coverage the changed code together
with the build, setup or input framing that would execute it and that the target
cannot produce; observable_consequence (J1c);
alternatives_ruled_out (J1d, D8), including counterevidence supplied with the
candidate; identity_preserved (J1e; for timing also T1).

Use effect blocked_target_coverage only for target_coverage. For target_coverage
fill every coverage_witness field; for the other class use empty strings. These
fields do not replace the citations of each check.

decision=keep requires all five checks established with source and harness
evidence, a finite_witness and no decisive missing fact. decision=dismiss requires
a grounded refutation or equivalent route (J6). Otherwise return uncertain and
request the missing facts (E3). For maintenance, unresolved timing does not
invalidate a currently established obligation (J2).

Then audit the whole scope again (J4): assess every selected change ID and the
four foundations with evidence, challenge both dismissals (J6) and retained
gaps (J1). Out-of-scope items (D7) are not obligations. New evidence contains
contracts retrieved for earlier requests (`evidence`); `citation_errors` lists earlier
citations the program could not verify; cite valid locations instead. Request
exact symbols or paths only for decisive missing facts (E3).
If the evidence establishes a distinct obligation that is NOT among `candidates`
(for example one that only became visible with the new evidence), report it in
`new_candidates` with the same fields as a discovery blocker and mark its change
group as blocked in the scope audit. It is reviewed in a separate turn; do not
add a decision for it here.

We use double-hashing,\n242|   since we can calculate a second hash value cheaply by taking those bits\n243|   of the first hash value that were discarded (masked out) when the table\n244|   index was calculated: index = hash & mask, where mask = table->size - 1.\n245|   We limit the maximum step size to table->size / 4 (mask >> 2) and make\n246|   it odd, since odd numbers are always relative prime to a power of 2.\n247|*/\n248|#define SECOND_HASH(hash, mask, power)                                         \\\n249|  ((((hash) & ~(mask)) >> ((power)-1)) & ((mask) >> 2))\n250|#define PROBE_STEP(hash, mask, power)                                          \\\n251|  ((unsigned char)((SECOND_HASH(hash, mask, power)) | 1))\n252|\n253|typedef struct {\n254|  NAMED **p;\n255|  NAMED **end;\n256|} HASH_TABLE_ITER;\n257|\n258|#define INIT_TAG_BUF_SIZE 32 /* must be a multiple of sizeof(XML_Char) */\n259|#define INIT_DATA_BUF_SIZE 1024\n260|#define INIT_ATTS_SIZE 16\n261|#define INIT_ATTS_VERSION 0xFFFFFFFF\n262|#define INIT_BLOCK_SIZE 1024\n263|#define INIT_BUFFER_SIZE 1024\n264|\n265|#define EXPAND_SPARE 24\n266|\n267|typedef struct binding {\n268|  struct prefix *prefix;\n269|  struct binding *nextTagBinding;\n270|  struct binding *prevPrefixBinding;\n271|  const struct attribute_id *attId;\n272|  XML_Char *uri;\n273|  int uriLen;\n274|  int uriAlloc;\n275|} BINDING;\n276|\n277|typedef struct prefix {\n278|  const XML_Char *name;\n279|  BINDING *binding;\n280|} PREFIX;\n281|\n282|typedef struct {\n283|  const XML_Char *str;\n284|  const XML_Char *localPart;\n285|  const XML_Char *prefix;\n286|  int strLen;\n287|  int uriLen;\n288|  int prefixLen;\n289|} TAG_NAME;\n290|\n291|/* TAG represents an open element.\n292|   The name of the element is stored in both the document and API\n293|   encodings. ret)\n7636|    return NULL;\n7637|\n7638|  /* What follows is an iterative implementation (of what was previously done\n7639|   * recursively in a dedicated function called \"build_node\".  The old recursive\n7640|   * build_node could be forced into stack exhaustion from input as small as a\n7641|   * few megabyte, and so that was a security issue.  Hence, a function call\n7642|   * stack is avoided now by resolving recursion.)\n7643|   *\n7644|   * The iterative approach works as follows:\n7645|   *\n7646|   * - We have two writing pointers, both walking up the result array; one does\n7647|   *   the work, the other creates \"jobs\" for its colleague to do, and leads\n7648|   *   the way:\n7649|   *\n7650|   *   - The faster one, pointer jobDest, always leads and writes \"what job\n7651|   *     to do\" by the other, once they reach that place in the\n7652|   *     array: leader \"jobDest\" stores the source node array index (relative\n7653|   *     to array dtd->scaffold) in field \"numchildren\".\n7654|   *\n7655|   *   - The slower one, pointer dest, looks at the value stored in the\n7656|   *     \"numchildren\" field (which actually holds a source node array index\n7657|   *     at that time) and puts the real data from dtd->scaffold in.\n7658|   *\n7659|   * - Before the loop starts, jobDest writes source array index 0\n7660|   *   (where the root node is located) so that dest will have something to do\n7661|   *   when it starts operation.\n7662|   *\n7663|   * - Whenever nodes with children are encountered, jobDest appends\n7664|   *   them as new jobs, in order.  As a result, tree node siblings are\n7665|   *   adjacent in the resulting array, for example:\n7666|   *\n7667|   *     [0] root, has two children\n7668|   *       [1] first child of 0, has three children\n7669|   *         [3] first child of 1, does not have children\n7670|   *         [4] second child of 1, does not have children\n7671|   *         [5] third child of 1, does not have children\n7672|   *       [2] second child of 0, does not have children\n7673|   *\n7674|   *   Or (the same data) presented in flat array view:\n7675|   *\n7676|   *     [0] root, has two children\n7677|   *\n7678|   *     [1] first child of 0, has three children\n7679|   *     [2] second child of 0, does not have children\n7680|   *\n7681|   *     [3] first child of 1, does not have children\n7682|   *     [4] second child of 1, does not have children\n7683|   *     [5] third child of 1, does not have children\n7684|   *\n7685|   * - The algorithm repeats until all target array indices have been processed.\n7686|   */\n7687|  XML_Content *dest = ret; /* tree node writing location, moves upwards */\n7688|  XML_Content *const destLimit = &ret[dtd->scaffCount];\n7689|  XML_Content *jobDest = ret; /* next free writing location in target array */\n7690|  str = (XML_Char *)&ret[dtd->scaffCount];\n7691|\n7692|  /* Add the starting job, the root node (index 0) of the source tree  */\n7693|  (jobDest++)->numchildren = 0;\n7694|\n7695|  for (; dest < destLimit; dest++) {\n7696|    /* Retrieve source tree array index from job storage */\n7697|    const int src_node = (int)dest->numchildren;\n7698|\n7699|    /* Convert item */\n7700|    dest->type = dtd->scaffold[src_node].type;\n7701|    dest->quant = dtd->scaffold[src_node].quant;\n7702|    if (dest->type == XML_CTYPE_NAME) {\n7703|      const XML_Char *src;\n7704|      dest->name = str;\n7705|      src = dtd->scaffold[src_node].name;\n7706|      for (;;) {\n7707|        *str++ = *src;\n7708|        if (! *src)\n7709|          break;\n7710|        src++;\n7711|      }\n7712|      dest->numchildren = 0;\n7713|      dest->children = NULL;\n7714|    } else {\n7715|      unsigned int i;\n7716|      int cn;\n7717|      dest->name = NULL;\n7718|      dest->numchildren = dtd->scaffold[src_node].childcnt;\n7719|      dest->children = jobDest;\n7720|\n7721|      /* Append scaffold indices of children to array */\n7722|      for (i = 0, cn = dtd->scaffold[src_node].firstchild;\n7723|           i < dest->numchildren; i++, cn = dtd->scaffold[cn].nextsib)\n7724|        (jobDest++)->numchildren = (unsigned int)cn;\n7725|    }\n7726|  }\n7727|\n7728|  return ret;\n7729|}\n7730|\n7731|static ELEMENT_TYPE *\n7732|getElementType(XML_Parser parser, const ENCODING *enc, const char *ptr,\n7733|               const char *end) {\n7734|  DTD *const dtd = parser->m_dtd; /* save one level of indirection */\n7735|  const XML_Char *name = poolStoreString(&dtd->pool, enc, ptr, end);\n7736|  ELEMENT_TYPE *ret;\n7737|\n7738|  if (!
